- **echo-quality-reviewer**: EchoReview quality lens. Scans a PR diff for the universal best-practices performance footguns and accessibility floors, and returns a JSON findings array. - **echo-reasoning-reviewer**: EchoReview reasoning lens. Runs the open-ended contradiction / unjustified-code pass over a PR diff at high confidence, and returns a JSON findings array. Invoked by echo-review when multi-agent mode is on and no reasoning-pass skip condition matched; one of five parallel reviewers. ([abdu4188/EchoReview](https://github.com/abdu4188/EchoReview/blob/HEAD/plugins/echoreview/agents/echo-reasoning-reviewer.md))

Invoked by echo-extract in multi-agent mode, one synthesizer per qualifying cluster; the orchestrator assigns IDs afterward. ([abdu4188/EchoReview](https://github.com/abdu4188/EchoReview/blob/HEAD/plugins/echoreview/agents/echo-rule-synthesizer.md))
